The Equations Python Generator Database (Signature Edition)

march to a million: in progress

Archive records: 48 / 1,000,000 target · 48 records (populated from the live archive index)

Specialist AI: JAH Equations Python Generator

JAH Equations Python Generator answers questions about this database, explains any record in plain sentences, compares records side by side on the same fields, and helps a reader follow the records in order. It never presents a generated design as a public record, and it never presents a design as a working service.

Boundless generator

The generator is seeded and deterministic, throttled to at most 100 records per call, duplicate-checked by deterministic hash ids, and hard-stopped at 1,000,000,000 records. Generator ceiling: up to 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files. Every generated record is labeled src "generated". Open the page script to use window.EquationsPythonGeneratorSignatureTools.

Validation engine

Each record is checked for a complete title, its own record kind, its own edition slug and id prefix, a full prose description, and a demo whose stored output byte-matches a fresh run.

Evidence links

  • Full record index for this edition (data-2)

Records

Generator coverage: Linear equations JAH-EQPYS-000001 · simple

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Linear equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Linear equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator linear equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Linear equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Linear equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Linear equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Quadratic equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7

        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)

        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000

                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),

            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)

    print(PythonGenerator().run())

    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Run 2 SHA-256: 5f3f01bb2be23aa3b0b2cf670c11aae76d695423e1eece71d3a41748ed1ae277

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Quadratic equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Cubic equations JAH-EQPYS-000003 · complex

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Cubic equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Cubic equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator cubic equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Cubic equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Cubic equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)

    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000

        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Trigonometric equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Logarithmic equations JAH-EQPYS-000005 · compounded

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Logarithmic equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Logarithmic equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator logarithmic equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Logarithmic equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)

    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000

        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Exponential equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Run 2 SHA-256: b745957cb7ff9b3f3c86d44cb5eee03ae86f0eed0790446c26151f48c6f6a42f

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Exponential equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Systems of equations JAH-EQPYS-000007 · simple

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Systems of equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Systems of equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator systems of equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Systems of equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():

    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'

    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

Formal equation

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Inequalities'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Run 1 SHA-256: 1dc288043d536955e530384ca2e90b00bedc9fc0e367f6000ba2f778b15c2bb0
Run 2 SHA-256: 1dc288043d536955e530384ca2e90b00bedc9fc0e367f6000ba2f778b15c2bb0
Deterministic: MATCH

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Inequalities'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Differential equations JAH-EQPYS-000009 · complex

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Differential equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Differential equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator differential equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Differential equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Differential equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed

        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'

        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

AI

JAH Equations Python Generator

Sources

Signature design-study: no public sources; design references are the companion pattern and the live database this pair companions.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator integral equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

Method

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

Dataset

The live database this pair companions, read from the local clone and the live manifest.

Verification path

python3 -c "import gzip,json,subprocess,sys; rows=[json.loads(l) for l in gzip.open('data/equations-python-generator-signature/index.jsonl.gz','rt')]; r=[x for x in rows if x['id']=='JAH-EQPYS-000010'][0]; open('/tmp/_gen.py','w').write(r['demo']['code']); print(subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/tmp/_gen.py'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout==r['expected_output'])"

Formal equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Integral equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Generated: {"checked": 24, "coverage_pct": 100.0, "covered": 24, "id": "JAH-EQPY-GEN-155433", "pass_rate_pct": 100.0, "passed": 24, "score": 100.0, "seed": 7, "title": "Generator coverage: Integral equations", "total": 24}
Run 1 SHA-256: 0a81340e418113aff1f2c8ef1302c7896d6d684035bbbde48eee426c657564f7
Run 2 SHA-256: 0a81340e418113aff1f2c8ef1302c7896d6d684035bbbde48eee426c657564f7
Deterministic: MATCH

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Integral equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Matrix equations JAH-EQPYS-000011 · compounded

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Matrix equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Matrix equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator matrix equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Matrix equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Matrix equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

AI

JAH Equations Python Generator

Sources

Signature design-study: no public sources; design references are the companion pattern and the live database this pair companions.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator complex-number equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

Method

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

Dataset

The live database this pair companions, read from the local clone and the live manifest.

Verification path

python3 -c "import gzip,json,subprocess,sys; rows=[json.loads(l) for l in gzip.open('data/equations-python-generator-signature/index.jsonl.gz','rt')]; r=[x for x in rows if x['id']=='JAH-EQPYS-000012'][0]; open('/tmp/_gen.py','w').write(r['demo']['code']); print(subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/tmp/_gen.py'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout==r['expected_output'])"

Formal equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Complex-number equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Run 1 SHA-256: d4078d167f613930697393f7cf2c48ec3c57450f702db90153dc2dbcdf1214c2
Run 2 SHA-256: d4078d167f613930697393f7cf2c48ec3c57450f702db90153dc2dbcdf1214c2

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Complex-number equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Polynomial equations JAH-EQPYS-000013 · simple

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Polynomial equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Polynomial equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator polynomial equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Polynomial equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()
