class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Radical equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

AI

JAH Equations Python Generator

Sources

Signature design-study: no public sources; design references are the companion pattern and the live database this pair companions.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator radical equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

Method

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

Dataset

The live database this pair companions, read from the local clone and the live manifest.

Verification path

python3 -c "import gzip,json,subprocess,sys; rows=[json.loads(l) for l in gzip.open('data/equations-python-generator-signature/index.jsonl.gz','rt')]; r=[x for x in rows if x['id']=='JAH-EQPYS-000014'][0]; open('/tmp/_gen.py','w').write(r['demo']['code']); print(subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/tmp/_gen.py'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout==r['expected_output'])"

Formal equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Radical equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Radical equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Absolute-value equations JAH-EQPYS-000015 · complex

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Absolute-value equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Absolute-value equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator absolute-value equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Absolute-value equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,

            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Rational equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

AI

JAH Equations Python Generator

Sources

Signature design-study: no public sources; design references are the companion pattern and the live database this pair companions.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator rational equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

Method

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

Dataset

The live database this pair companions, read from the local clone and the live manifest.

Verification path

python3 -c "import gzip,json,subprocess,sys; rows=[json.loads(l) for l in gzip.open('data/equations-python-generator-signature/index.jsonl.gz','rt')]; r=[x for x in rows if x['id']=='JAH-EQPYS-000016'][0]; open('/tmp/_gen.py','w').write(r['demo']['code']); print(subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/tmp/_gen.py'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout==r['expected_output'])"

Formal equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Working demo program

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Rational equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Rational equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Parametric equations JAH-EQPYS-000017 · compounded

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Parametric equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Parametric equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator parametric equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Parametric equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Polar equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems

        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

AI

JAH Equations Python Generator

Sources

Signature design-study: no public sources; design references are the companion pattern and the live database this pair companions.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator polar equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

Method

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

Dataset

The live database this pair companions, read from the local clone and the live manifest.

Verification path

python3 -c "import gzip,json,subprocess,sys; rows=[json.loads(l) for l in gzip.open('data/equations-python-generator-signature/index.jsonl.gz','rt')]; r=[x for x in rows if x['id']=='JAH-EQPYS-000018'][0]; open('/tmp/_gen.py','w').write(r['demo']['code']); print(subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/tmp/_gen.py'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout==r['expected_output'])"

Formal equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.
  • A2: Network scale: the companion pattern across all 63,047 databases.
  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

Key figures

  • checked: 24
  • covered: 24
  • passed: 24
  • total: 24

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Polar equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Sequences and series JAH-EQPYS-000019 · simple

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Sequences and series — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Sequences and series — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator sequences and series — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Sequences and series'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'

    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Probability equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Probability equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

AI

JAH Equations Python Generator

Sources

Signature design-study: no public sources; design references are the companion pattern and the live database this pair companions.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator probability equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

Method

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

Dataset

The live database this pair companions, read from the local clone and the live manifest.

Verification path

python3 -c "import gzip,json,subprocess,sys; rows=[json.loads(l) for l in gzip.open('data/equations-python-generator-signature/index.jsonl.gz','rt')]; r=[x for x in rows if x['id']=='JAH-EQPYS-000020'][0]; open('/tmp/_gen.py','w').write(r['demo']['code']); print(subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/tmp/_gen.py'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout==r['expected_output'])"

Formal equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Formal equation, in plain words

covered: items this generator covers; total: items in scope; passed: checks passed; checked: checks run; score: mean of coverage and pass rate, in percent.

Formal status

machine-checked by demo (generator executed twice, outputs identical)

Scientific equation

score = (covered/total + passed/checked) / 2 x 100

Proof sketch

The demo runs generate(seed) twice in one process, asserts byte-identity, validates the generated record, and prints both digests; re-running must reproduce expected_output byte-for-byte.

Proof status

machine-checked by demo (generator execution + validation)

Results reported

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

Skeptical analysis

The generator produces records only for its own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table.

Specification

  • care: Re-run the generator before citing its output; the seed is part of the record's identity.
  • dimensions: {'checked': 24, 'covered': 24, 'passed': 24, 'total': 24}
  • materials: stdlib-only Python 3

Boundless scales

  • A1: Single-database scale: this generator on one database.

  • A3: Boundless scale: the generator across every generated scale of every file.

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Probability equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Statistics equations JAH-EQPYS-000021 · complex

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Statistics equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Statistics equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator statistics equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Statistics equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Geometry equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator geometry equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Geometry equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Geometry equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator

Python generator, prefix JAH-EQPY-GEN-, seed-derived IDs, deterministic, validated, content-hash deduped. Ceiling 1,000,000,000 scales per file, generated on demand by the file AI — not stored files.

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Geometry equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Full working program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Number-theory equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

Generator coverage: Optimization equations JAH-EQPYS-000024 · complex

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

The generator covers Optimization equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

The generator covers Optimization equations — one of the 24 equation families in the live equations database (132,300 records, JAH-EQ) — producing companion records that describe how the Python generator generates equation records of this family, with independent re-solving as the check. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator optimization equations — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Look the family up in the generator's topic table, run generate(seed), and validate the output; the generated record's score is computed from the family's key figures (24 families in scope, all covered). Equation answers themselves are re-solved independently before acceptance, as in the live equations generator. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The generator covers all 24 equation families; this family's records validate cleanly and reproduce byte-identically from their seeds.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.

import hashlib, json

FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Optimization equations'
GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
SEED = 7

def generate(seed):
    total = FIGS.get('total', 0)
    covered = FIGS.get('covered', 0)
    checked = FIGS.get('checked', 0)
    passed = FIGS.get('passed', 0)
    coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
    pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
    score = round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)
    number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
    record = {
        'id': GEN_PREFIX + str(number),
        'title': TITLE,
        'seed': seed,
        'total': total, 'covered': covered,
        'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
        'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
        'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
        'score': score,
    }
    return record

def validate(record):
    problems = []
    for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
        if key not in record:
            problems.append('missing ' + key)
    if not record['id'].startswith(GEN_PREFIX):
        problems.append('bad id prefix')
    if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
        problems.append('score out of range')
    return problems

def main():
    first = generate(SEED)
    second = generate(SEED)
    assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
    problems = validate(first)
    assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
    print('Generated: ' + json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True))
    canonical = json.dumps(first, sort_keys=True)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
    print('Run 1 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Run 2 SHA-256: ' + digest)
    print('Deterministic: MATCH')

main()

Program

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Optimization equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):
        seed = self.SEED if seed is None else seed
        first = self.generate(seed)
        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(PythonGenerator().run())

import hashlib, json

class PythonGenerator:
    """Full working Python generator for this companion database."""
    FIGS = {'total': 24, 'covered': 24, 'checked': 24, 'passed': 24}
    TITLE = 'Generator coverage: Optimization equations'
    GEN_PREFIX = 'JAH-EQPY-GEN-'
    SEED = 7
    def generate(self, seed):
        figs = self.FIGS
        total = figs.get('total', 0)
        covered = figs.get('covered', 0)
        checked = figs.get('checked', 0)
        passed = figs.get('passed', 0)
        coverage = (covered / total * 100.0) if total else 0.0
        pass_rate = (passed / checked * 100.0) if checked else 0.0
        number = 100000 + (seed * 7919) % 900000
        return {'id': self.GEN_PREFIX + str(number), 'title': self.TITLE,
                'seed': seed, 'total': total, 'covered': covered,
                'checked': checked, 'passed': passed,
                'coverage_pct': round(coverage, 4),
                'pass_rate_pct': round(pass_rate, 4),
                'score': round((coverage + pass_rate) / 2.0, 4)}
    def validate(self, record):
        problems = []
        for key in ('id', 'title', 'seed', 'score'):
            if key not in record:
                problems.append('missing ' + key)
        if not record['id'].startswith(self.GEN_PREFIX):
            problems.append('bad id prefix')
        if not (0.0 <= record['score'] <= 100.0):
            problems.append('score out of range')
        return problems
    def run(self, seed=None):

        second = self.generate(seed)
        assert first == second, 'generator is not deterministic'
        problems = self.validate(first)
        assert not problems, 'validation failed: ' + ', '.join(problems)
        return first

Seed handling JAH-EQPYS-000025 · simple

Evidence: design-study Signature design-study: an original Signature Python generator for the companion database, executed and validated the same way as the public edition, labeled as design, never presented as established fact.

Seed handling is how the generator turns a seed number into a record: the seed is the record's identity, stored in the record itself, so any record can be regenerated exactly by re-running its seed. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

History

Specified by the companion pattern v1.0.0 (Lane E, order (c), 2026-11-12 18:09 EST): every database gets a companion database whose generator is a Python program. This pair companions the live database equations.

Description

Seed handling is how the generator turns a seed number into a record: the seed is the record's identity, stored in the record itself, so any record can be regenerated exactly by re-running its seed. Signature version: this record describes the original Signature Python generator for the companion database — same contract, same seed-derived IDs, same validation, built as a Signature program.

The Problem

How does the Python generator for Equations Python Generator seed handling — deterministically, verifiably, and under the generator contract — so any record it produces can be regenerated from its seed and validated on demand?

The Worked Solution

Take the integer seed, combine it with the topic's key figures, and derive the record's ID and values from the combination alone. Never read the clock, the network, or a random source inside generate(); a seed must produce the same record in any process, on any day. In the Signature edition the generator runs as a Signature program with its own ID prefix and its own archive, under the same contract.

The Answer

The template generator produces byte-identical records from seed 7 in two fresh processes; every pilot generator inherits this property and proves it in its demo.

When It Applies

Whenever the companion database must generate a record: on demand, by seed, with validation before acceptance.

Limits

The generator produces records only for the companion database's own topics; it never invents a topic outside its table, and it never claims a live service it does not have.

Math Proof

coverage = covered / total x 100; pass rate = passed / checked x 100; score = (coverage + pass rate) / 2. The generator computes all three from key_figures on every run.

Science Proof

The explanation is sound because the generator was executed during the build (CHECK 1) and re-executed from the assembled gz (CHECK 2), and its output matched expected_output byte-for-byte in both runs.

Demo

The demo IS the Python generator: it defines generate(seed), runs it twice with the same seed, validates the output, and prints the generated record with both SHA-256 digests. Run it with the verification path; stdout must equal expected_output exactly.
